Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a chemical algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 242. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 101/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 242 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the chemical structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 101. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
